@dataclass
class WsDailySumRec:
    """Daily summary record data."""
    daily_month: int = 0
    daily_trans_count: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    daily_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def sum_month_data(ws_monthly_summary, ws_curr_month) -> None:
    """Sum month data."""
    logger.info("Summing month data")
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = Decimal("0")
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal("0")
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = Decimal("0")
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = Decimal("0")
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    daily_summary_file = []
    for i in range(1, 32):
        daily_data = WsDailySumRec(daily_month=ws_curr_month, daily_trans_count=Decimal(i * 10), daily_trans_amount=Decimal(i * 100))
        daily_summary_file.append(daily_data)

    index = 0
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file[index]
            index += 1

            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_month == ws_curr_month:
                ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
        except IndexError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi() -> None:
    """Calculate KPI."""
    logger.info("Calculating KPI")
    calc_financial_kpi()
    calc_operational_kpi()
    calc_customer_kpi()

def calc_financial_kpi() -> None:
    """Calculate financial KPI."""
    logger.info("Calculating financial KPI")
    ws_total_assets = Decimal("1000000")
    ws_net_income = Decimal("100000")
    ws_total_equity = Decimal("500000")
    ws_interest_expense = Decimal("10000")
    ws_interest_income = Decimal("20000")
    ws_earning_assets = Decimal("1500000")

    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100
    else:
        ws_roa = Decimal("0")

    if ws_total_equity > 0:
        ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100
    else:
        ws_roe = Decimal("0")

    if ws_interest_expense > 0:
        ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100
    else:
        ws_nim = Decimal("0")

def calc_operational_kpi() -> None:
    """Calculate operational KPI."""
    logger.info("Calculating operational KPI")
    ws_total_trans_count = Decimal("1000")
    ws_error_count = Decimal("10")
    ws_within_sla_count = Decimal("90")
    ws_total_cases = Decimal("100")
    ws_fcr_count = Decimal("80")
    ws_total_calls = Decimal("100")

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    else:
        ws_error_rate = Decimal("0")

    ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi() -> None:
    """Calculate customer KPI."""
    logger.info("Calculating customer KPI")
    ws_active_customers = Decimal("1000")
    ws_churned_customers = Decimal("100")
    ws_marketing_spend = Decimal("10000")
    ws_new_customers = Decimal("50")
    ws_avg_revenue_per_customer = Decimal("100")
    ws_avg_customer_tenure = Decimal("5")

    if ws_active_customers > 0:
        ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100
    else:
        ws_churn_rate = Decimal("0")

    ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure

def generate_dashboard() -> None:
    """Generate dashboard."""
    logger.info("Generating dashboard")
    create_executive_dashboard()
    create_operations_dashboard()
    create_risk_dashboard()

@dataclass
class WsDailySumRec:
    """Daily summary record."""
    daily_date: str = ""
    daily_trans_count: str = ""
    daily_trans_amount: str = ""
    daily_deposits: str = ""
    daily_withdrawals: str = ""

@dataclass
class WsAccountRec:
    """Account record."""
    acct_last_activity: str = ""
    acct_status: str = ""
    acct_status_desc: str = ""
    acct_dormant_date: str = ""

def export_csv(ws_eof_flag: str, csv_record: str, ws_csv_header: str, ws_csv_line: str, daily_date: str, daily_trans_count: str, daily_trans_amount: str, daily_deposits: str, daily_withdrawals: str) -> str:
    """Exports data to CSV file."""
    logger.info("Executing export_csv")
    ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    csv_record = ws_csv_header
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        ws_daily_sum_rec = WsDailySumRec() # Assuming this is populated from somewhere
        if True: # Assuming read success, replace with actual read logic
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_csv_line = daily_date + ',' + daily_trans_count + ',' + daily_trans_amount + ',' + daily_deposits + ',' + daily_withdrawals
            csv_record = ws_csv_line
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    return ws_eof_flag

def export_xml(ws_eof_flag: str, xml_record: str, ws_xml_line: str, ws_daily_sum_rec: WsDailySumRec, daily_date: str, daily_trans_count: str) -> str:
    """Exports data to XML file."""
    logger.info("Executing export_xml")
    ws_xml_line = '<?xml version="1.0"?>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    ws_xml_line = '<DailySummaries>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    ws_xml_line, ws_eof_flag = write_xml_records(ws_eof_flag, xml_record, ws_xml_line, ws_daily_sum_rec, daily_date, daily_trans_count)
    ws_xml_line = '</DailySummaries>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    return ws_xml_line, ws_eof_flag

def write_xml_records(ws_eof_flag: str, xml_record: str, ws_xml_line: str, ws_daily_sum_rec: WsDailySumRec, daily_date: str, daily_trans_count: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Writes XML records."""
    logger.info("Executing write_xml_records")
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        if True: # Replace with actual read logic
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_xml_line, xml_record = format_xml_record(ws_xml_line, xml_record, daily_date, daily_trans_count)
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    return ws_xml_line, ws_eof_flag

def format_xml_record(ws_xml_line: str, xml_record: str, daily_date: str, daily_trans_count: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Formats a single XML record."""
    logger.info("Executing format_xml_record")
    ws_xml_line = '<Summary>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    ws_xml_line = '<Date>' + daily_date + '</Date>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    ws_xml_line = '<TransCount>' + daily_trans_count + '</TransCount>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    ws_xml_line = '</Summary>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    return ws_xml_line, xml_record

def export_json(ws_json_line: str, json_record: str, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_daily_sum_rec: WsDailySumRec, daily_date: str, daily_trans_count: str, daily_trans_amount: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Exports data to JSON file."""
    logger.info("Executing export_json")
    ws_json_line = '{"dailySummaries":['
    json_record = ws_json_line
    ws_json_line, ws_eof_flag = write_json_records(ws_json_line, json_record, ws_eof_flag, ws_daily_sum_rec, daily_date, daily_trans_count, daily_trans_amount)
    ws_json_line = ']}'
    json_record = ws_json_line
    return ws_json_line, ws_eof_flag

def write_json_records(ws_json_line: str, json_record: str, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_daily_sum_rec: WsDailySumRec, daily_date: str, daily_trans_count: str, daily_trans_amount: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Writes JSON records."""
    logger.info("Executing write_json_records")
    ws_first_record = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        if True: # Replace with actual read logic
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_json_line, json_record, ws_first_record = format_json_record(ws_json_line, json_record, ws_first_record, daily_date, daily_trans_count, daily_trans_amount)
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    return ws_json_line, ws_eof_flag

def format_json_record(ws_json_line: str, json_record: str, ws_first_record: str, daily_date: str, daily_trans_count: str, daily_trans_amount: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """Formats a single JSON record."""
    logger.info("Executing format_json_record")
    if ws_first_record == 'Y':
        ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        ws_json_comma = ' '
        ws_first_record = 'Y'
    ws_json_line = ws_json_comma + '{"date":"' + daily_date + '","transCount":' + daily_trans_count + ',"transAmount":' + daily_trans_amount + '}'
    json_record = ws_json_line
    return ws_json_line, json_record, ws_first_record

def account_maintenance(ws_account_rec: WsAccountRec, ws_process_date: str, account_record: str, acct_status: str, acct_status_desc: str, acct_dormant_date: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str, ws_eof_flag: str) -> None:
    """Performs account maintenance procedures."""
    logger.info("Executing account_maintenance")
    dormant_account_check(ws_account_rec, ws_process_date, account_record, ws_eof_flag)
    escheatment_processing(ws_account_rec, account_record, acct_status, ws_eof_flag)
    account_closure()
    account_reactivation()

def dormant_account_check(ws_account_rec: WsAccountRec, ws_process_date: str, account_record: str, ws_eof_flag: str) -> str:
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    logger.info("Executing dormant_account_check")
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        ws_account_rec = WsAccountRec()
        if True: # Replace with actual read logic
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_account_rec, account_record = check_activity(ws_account_rec, ws_process_date, account_record)
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    return account_record

def check_activity(ws_account_rec: WsAccountRec, ws_process_date: str, account_record: str) -> tuple[WsAccountRec, str]:
    """Checks account activity and marks dormant if necessary."""
    logger.info("Executing check_activity")
    ws_days_inactive = int(ws_process_date) - int(ws_account_rec.acct_last_activity)
    if ws_days_inactive > 365:
        ws_account_rec.acct_status = 'D'
        ws_account_rec, account_record = mark_dormant(ws_account_rec, ws_process_date, account_record)
    return ws_account_rec, account_record

def mark_dormant(ws_account_rec: WsAccountRec, ws_process_date: str, account_record: str) -> tuple[WsAccountRec, str]:
    """Marks an account as dormant."""
    logger.info("Executing mark_dormant")
    ws_account_rec.acct_status_desc = 'DORMANT'
    ws_account_rec.acct_dormant_date = ws_process_date
    account_record = str(ws_account_rec)
    send_dormant_notice()
    return ws_account_rec, account_record

def send_dormant_notice() -> None:
    """Sends a dormant account notice."""
    logger.info("Executing send_dormant_notice")
    ws_notif_type = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification()

def check_escheatment(ws_process_date: date, acct_dormant_date: date, ws_escheat_years: int) -> None:
    """22210-check_escheatment."""
    logger.info("Executing 22210-check_escheatment")
    ws_dormant_years = (ws_process_date.toordinal() - acct_dormant_date.toordinal()) / 365
    if ws_dormant_years >= ws_escheat_years:
        escheat_account()

def account_closure(ws_close_request: str) -> None:
    """22300-account_closure."""
    logger.info("Executing 22300-account_closure")
    if ws_close_request == 'Y':
        validate_closure()
        if WS_CLOSURE_VALID == 'Y':
            process_closure()
        else:
            reject_closure()

def validate_closure() -> None:
    """22310-validate_closure."""
    logger.info("Executing 22310-validate_closure")
    global WS_CLOSURE_VALID, WS_CLOSURE_REJECT, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_PENDING_TRANS, ACCT_LOAN_LINK
    WS_CLOSURE_VALID = 'Y'
    if ACCT_BALANCE < 0:
        WS_CLOSURE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_CLOSURE_REJECT = 'NEGATIVE BALANCE'
    if ACCT_PENDING_TRANS > 0:
        WS_CLOSURE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_CLOSURE_REJECT = 'PENDING TRANSACTIONS'
    if ACCT_LOAN_LINK != " ":
        WS_CLOSURE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_CLOSURE_REJECT = 'LINKED LOAN EXISTS'

def process_closure() -> None:
    """22320-process_closure."""
    logger.info("Executing 22320-process_closure")
    global WS_FINAL_BALANCE, ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_STATUS, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_FINAL_BALANCE  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_BALANCE
    disburse_balance()
    ACCT_STATUS = 'C'
    ACCT_CLOSE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    rewrite_account_record()
    archive_account()

    """22410-validate_reactivation."""
    logger.info("Executing 22410-validate_reactivation")
    global WS_REACT_VALID, WS_REACT_REJECT, ACCT_STATUS, WS_DAYS_SINCE_CLOSE

        WS_REACT_VALID = 'N'

            WS_REACT_VALID = 'N'
            WS_REACT_REJECT = 'CLOSURE PERIOD EXCEEDED'

def calculate_luhn_check() -> None:
    """Calculate Luhn check digit."""
    logger.info("Calculating Luhn check digit")
    ws_luhn_sum = 0
    for ws_luhn_idx in range(15, 0, -1):
        ws_luhn_digit = int(ws_card_number_temp[ws_luhn_idx - 1])
        if (16 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        ws_luhn_sum += ws_luhn_digit
    global ws_luhn_check
    ws_luhn_check = (10 - (ws_luhn_sum % 10)) % 10

def set_card_limits() -> None:
    """Set card limits based on card type."""
    logger.info("Setting card limits")
    global ws_daily_limit, ws_atm_limit
    if ws_card_type == 'DEBIT':
        ws_daily_limit = Decimal("1000")
        ws_atm_limit = Decimal("500")
    elif ws_card_type == 'CREDIT':
        ws_daily_limit = ws_credit_line
        ws_atm_limit = ws_credit_line * Decimal("0.2")
    elif ws_card_type == 'PREMIUM':
        ws_daily_limit = Decimal("10000")
        ws_atm_limit = Decimal("2000")

def assign_network() -> None:
    """Assign card network based on card prefix."""
    logger.info("Assigning card network")
    global ws_card_network
    if ws_card_prefix == '4':
        ws_card_network = 'VISA'
    elif ws_card_prefix == '5':
        ws_card_network = 'MASTERCARD'
    elif ws_card_prefix == '3':
        ws_card_network = 'AMEX'
    else:
        ws_card_network = 'DISCOVER'

def create_card_record() -> None:
    """Create a card record."""
    logger.info("Creating card record")
    global card_expiry_date
    card_record.card_number = ws_card_number
    card_record.card_type = ws_card_type
    card_record.card_network = ws_card_network
    card_record.card_daily_limit = ws_daily_limit
    card_record.card_atm_limit = ws_atm_limit
    card_expiry_date = int(ws_process_date) + 1095
    card_record.card_status = 'I'
    # Assuming write_card_record is a defined function for writing the record
    write_card_record(card_record)

def card_activation() -> None:
    """Process card activation request."""
    logger.info("Processing card activation")
    if ws_activation_request == 'Y':
        verify_cardholder()
        if ws_cardholder_verified == 'Y':
            activate_card()
        else:
            activation_failed()

def verify_cardholder() -> None:
    pass  # auto-added
# UNINDENT: from dataclasses import dataclass

def verify_cardholder_details():
    """Verify cardholder details."""
    logger.info("Verifying cardholder details")
    global ws_cardholder_verified
    ws_cardholder_verified = 'N'
    if ws_cvv_input == ws_card_cvv:
        if ws_dob_input == ws_cardholder_dob:
            if ws_ssn_last4_input == ws_cardholder_ssn_last4:
                ws_cardholder_verified = 'Y'

def activate_card() -> None:
    """Activate the card."""
    logger.info("Activating card")
    card_record.card_status = 'A'
    card_record.card_activation_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record) # Assuming rewrite_card_record function is defined
    ws_notif_type = 'card_activated'
    ws_notif_channel = 'SMS'
    ws_notif_body = 'Your card is now active'
    send_notification() # Assuming send_notification is a defined function

def activation_failed() -> None:
    """Handle failed activation attempt."""
    logger.info("Handling failed activation attempt")
    global ws_activation_attempts
    ws_activation_attempts += 1
    if ws_activation_attempts >= 3:
        card_blocking() # Assuming card_blocking function is defined
    ws_notif_type = 'activation_failed'
    send_notification() # Assuming send_notification is a defined function

        if ws_pin_valid == 'Y':
            set_new_pin() # Assuming set_new_pin function is defined

def validate_current_pin(ws_variables: WsVariables, card_record: WsCardRecord) -> None:
    """Validates the current PIN."""
    logger.info("Executing 23310-validate_current_pin")
    ws_variables.ws_pin_valid = 'N'
    pinverify_result = pinverify(ws_variables.ws_card_number, ws_variables.ws_current_pin)
    ws_variables.ws_pin_verify_result = pinverify_result
    if ws_variables.ws_pin_verify_result == 'MATCH':
        ws_variables.ws_pin_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_variables.ws_pin_attempts += 1
        if ws_variables.ws_pin_attempts >= 3:
            card_blocking(ws_variables, card_record)

def set_new_pin(ws_variables: WsVariables, card_record: WsCardRecord) -> None:
    """Sets a new PIN."""
    logger.info("Executing 23320-set_new_pin")
    encrypted_pin = pinenrypt(ws_variables.ws_new_pin)
    ws_variables.ws_encrypted_pin = encrypted_pin
    card_record.card_pin_block = ws_variables.ws_encrypted_pin
    card_record.card_pin_change_date = ws_variables.ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record)
    ws_variables.ws_notif_type = 'pin_changed'
    ws_variables.ws_notif_channel = 'SMS'
    ws_variables.ws_notif_body = 'Your PIN has been changed'
    send_notification(ws_variables)

def card_replacement(ws_variables: WsVariables, card_record: WsCardRecord) -> None:
    """Handles card replacement."""
    logger.info("Executing 23400-card_replacement")
    if ws_variables.ws_replace_request == 'Y':
        cancel_old_card(ws_variables, card_record)
        card_issuance(ws_variables, card_record)
        ship_new_card(ws_variables)

def cancel_old_card(ws_variables: WsVariables, card_record: WsCardRecord) -> None:
    """Cancels the old card."""
    logger.info("Executing 23410-cancel_old_card")
    card_record.card_status = 'R'
    card_record.card_cancel_reason = 'REPLACED'
    card_record.card_cancel_date = ws_variables.ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record)

def ship_new_card(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Ships the new card."""
    logger.info("Executing 23420-ship_new_card")
    shipment_record = WsShipmentRecord()
    shipment_record.ship_card_number = ws_variables.ws_card_number
    shipment_record.ship_address = ws_variables.ws_cardholder_address
    if ws_variables.ws_expedite == 'Y':
        shipment_record.ship_method = 'EXPRESS'
        shipment_record.ship_est_delivery = Decimal(str(integer_of_date(ws_variables.ws_process_date) + 2))
    else:
        shipment_record.ship_method = 'STANDARD'
        shipment_record.ship_est_delivery = Decimal(str(integer_of_date(ws_variables.ws_process_date) + 7))
    write_shipment_record(shipment_record)

def card_blocking(ws_variables: WsVariables, card_record: WsCardRecord) -> None:
    """Blocks the card."""
    logger.info("Executing 23500-card_blocking")
    card_record.card_status = 'B'
    card_record.card_block_reason = ws_variables.ws_block_reason
    card_record.card_block_date = ws_variables.ws_process_date
    rewrite_card_record(card_record)
    ws_variables.ws_notif_type = 'card_blocked'
    ws_variables.ws_notif_channel = 'SMS'
# SYNTAX:     ws_variables.ws_notif_body = f\'Your card has been blocked: {ws_variables.ws_block_reason}''
    send_notification(ws_variables)

def wire_transfer(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Initiates wire transfer procedure."""
    logger.info("Executing 24000-wire_transfer")
    validate_wire_request(ws_variables)
    if ws_variables.ws_wire_valid == 'Y':
        ofac_screening(ws_variables)
        if ws_variables.ws_ofac_clear == 'Y':
            process_wire(ws_variables)
            send_confirmation(ws_variables)
        else:
            reject_wire(ws_variables)

def validate_wire_request(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Validates the wire transfer request."""
    logger.info("Executing 24100-validate_wire_request")
    ws_variables.ws_wire_valid = 'Y'
    if ws_variables.ws_wire_amount <= 0:
        ws_variables.ws_wire_valid = 'N'
        ws_variables.ws_wire_reject = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if ws_variables.ws_wire_amount > ws_variables.ws_account_balance:
        ws_variables.ws_wire_valid = 'N'
        ws_variables.ws_wire_reject = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if ws_variables.ws_beneficiary_account == " ":
        ws_variables.ws_wire_valid = 'N'
        ws_variables.ws_wire_reject = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if ws_variables.ws_wire_amount > 10000:
        ws_variables.ws_ctr_required = 'Y'

def ofac_screening(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Screens the wire transfer against OFAC."""
    logger.info("Executing 24200-ofac_screening")
    ws_variables.ws_ofac_clear = 'Y'
    ofac_request = OfacRequest()
    ofac_response = OfacResponse()
    ofac_request.ofac_search_name = ws_variables.ws_beneficiary_name
    ofac_search(ofac_request, ofac_response)
    if ofac_response.ofac_match_found == 'Y':
        if ofac_response.ofac_match_score >= 85:
            ws_variables.ws_ofac_clear = 'N'
            ws_variables.ws_wire_reject = 'OFAC MATCH'
    ofac_request.ofac_search_bank = ws_variables.ws_beneficiary_bank
    ofac_search(ofac_request, ofac_response)
    if ofac_response.ofac_match_found == 'Y':
        if ofac_response.ofac_match_score >= 85:
            ws_variables.ws_ofac_clear = 'N'
            ws_variables.ws_wire_reject = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Processes the wire transfer."""
    logger.info("Executing 24300-process_wire")
    debit_originator(ws_variables)
    create_wire_message(ws_variables)
    transmit_wire(ws_variables)
    record_wire(ws_variables)

def debit_originator(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Debits the originator\'s account."""
    logger.info("Executing 24310-debit_originator")
    ws_variables.ws_account_balance -= ws_variables.ws_wire_amount
    ws_variables.ws_account_balance -= ws_variables.ws_wire_fee
    update_account(ws_variables)

def create_wire_message(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Creates the SWIFT wire message."""
    logger.info("Executing 24320-create_wire_message")
    swift_message = SwiftMessage()
    swift_message.swift_msg_type = 'MT103'
    swift_message.swift_txn_ref = ws_variables.ws_wire_ref
    swift_message.swift_value_date = ws_variables.ws_wire_date
    swift_message.swift_currency = ws_variables.ws_wire_currency
    swift_message.swift_amount = ws_variables.ws_wire_amount
    swift_message.swift_ordering_cust = ws_variables.ws_originator_name
    swift_message.swift_ordering_acct = ws_variables.ws_originator_account
    swift_message.swift_benef_cust = ws_variables.ws_beneficiary_name
    swift_message.swift_benef_acct = ws_variables.ws_beneficiary_account
    swift_message.swift_benef_bank = ws_variables.ws_beneficiary_bank
    swift_message.swift_remit_info = ws_variables.ws_purpose
    ws_variables.ws_swift_message = str(swift_message)

def transmit_wire(ws_variables: WsVariables) -> None:
    """Transmits the wire via SWIFT."""
    logger.info("Executing 24330-transmit_wire")
    swift_response = swiftsend(ws_variables.ws_swift_message)
    ws_variables.ws_swift_response = swift_response
    if "ACK" in swift_response:
        ws_variables.ws_wire_status = 'SENT'
    else:
        ws_variables.ws_wire_status = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit(ws_variables)

interest_record: str = "" # Assuming this is a file

ws_interest_record = WsInterestRecord() # Instance of dataclass

def interest_accrual(account: AccountData) -> None:
    """Calculate and accrue interest."""
    logger.info("Executing interest_accrual")
    calculate_daily_interest(account)
    accrue_interest()
    post_monthly_interest(account)

def calculate_daily_interest(account: AccountData) -> None:
    """Calculate daily interest based on account type."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_daily_interest")
    if account.acct_type == 'SAV':
        savings_interest()
    elif account.acct_type == 'MMA':
        money_market_interest()
    elif account.acct_type == 'CD':
        cd_interest(account)
    elif account.acct_type == 'CHK':
        if account.acct_interest_bearing == 'Y':
            checking_interest()

def savings_interest() -> None:
    """Calculate savings account interest."""
    logger.info("Executing savings_interest")
    global ws_daily_interest
    if ws_account_balance >= 0:
        determine_savings_tier()
        ws_daily_interest = ws_account_balance * ws_tier_rate / Decimal("36500")
    else:
        ws_daily_interest = Decimal("0")

def determine_savings_tier() -> None:
    """Determine savings tier based on account balance."""
    logger.info("Executing determine_savings_tier")
    global ws_tier_rate
    if ws_account_balance >= Decimal("100000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("2.50")
    elif ws_account_balance >= Decimal("50000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("2.00")
    elif ws_account_balance >= Decimal("10000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("1.50")
    elif ws_account_balance >= Decimal("1000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("1.00")
    else:
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("0.50")

def money_market_interest() -> None:
    """Calculate money market account interest."""
    logger.info("Executing money_market_interest")

ws_account_balance = Decimal("0")

ws_daily_interest = Decimal("0")
ws_min_bal_for_interest = Decimal("0")

def s_daily_interest():
    global ws_daily_interest
    if ws_account_balance >= 0:
        determine_mma_tier()
        ws_daily_interest = ws_account_balance * ws_tier_rate / Decimal("36500")
    else:
        ws_daily_interest = Decimal("0")

def determine_mma_tier() -> None:
    """Determine MMA tier based on account balance."""
    logger.info("Executing determine_mma_tier")
    global ws_tier_rate
    if ws_account_balance >= Decimal("250000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("3.50")
    elif ws_account_balance >= Decimal("100000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("3.00")
    elif ws_account_balance >= Decimal("50000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("2.50")
    elif ws_account_balance >= Decimal("25000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("2.00")
    elif ws_account_balance >= Decimal("10000"):
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("1.50")
    else:
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("1.00")

def cd_interest(account: AccountData) -> None:
    """Calculate CD account interest."""
    logger.info("Executing cd_interest")
    global ws_daily_interest, ws_tier_rate
    if ws_account_balance > 0:
        ws_tier_rate = account.acct_cd_rate
        ws_daily_interest = ws_account_balance * ws_tier_rate / Decimal("36500")

def checking_interest() -> None:
    """Calculate checking account interest."""
    logger.info("Executing checking_interest")
    global ws_daily_interest, ws_tier_rate
    if ws_account_balance >= ws_min_bal_for_interest:
        ws_tier_rate = Decimal("0.10")
        ws_daily_interest = ws_account_balance * ws_tier_rate / Decimal("36500")
    else:
        ws_daily_interest = Decimal("0")

def accrue_interest() -> None:
    """Accrue daily interest."""
    logger.info("Executing accrue_interest")
    global ws_accrued_interest, ws_last_accrual_date
    ws_accrued_interest += ws_daily_interest
    ws_last_accrual_date = ws_process_date

def post_monthly_interest(account: AccountData) -> None:
    """Post monthly interest to account balance."""
    logger.info("Executing post_monthly_interest")
    global ws_account_balance, ws_accrued_interest
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_account_balance += ws_accrued_interest
        record_interest_posting(account)
        ws_accrued_interest = Decimal("0")

def record_interest_posting(account: AccountData) -> None:
    """Record interest posting details."""
    logger.info("Executing record_interest_posting")
    global ws_interest_record, interest_record
    ws_interest_record = WsInterestRecord() # Re-initialize the dataclass instance
    ws_interest_record.int_account = account.acct_id
    ws_interest_record.int_amount = ws_accrued_interest
    ws_interest_record.int_rate = ws_tier_rate
    ws_interest_record.int_post_date = ws_process_date
    # Assuming 'WRITE interest_record FROM ws_interest_record' translates to writing to a file
    with open(interest_record, 'a') as f:
        pass

def stop_payment() -> None:
    """29000-stop_payment."""
    logger.info("Executing stop_payment")
    validate_stop_request()
    if ws_stop_valid == 'Y':
        create_stop_order()
        apply_stop_fee()

def validate_stop_request() -> None:
    """29100-validate_stop_request."""
    logger.info("Executing validate_stop_request")
    global ws_stop_valid, ws_stop_reject
    ws_stop_valid = 'Y'
    if ws_check_number == Decimal("0"):
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if ws_check_already_cleared == 'Y':
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def create_stop_order() -> None:
    """29200-create_stop_order."""
    logger.info("Executing create_stop_order")
    global ws_stop_record
    ws_stop_record = WsStopRecord()
    ws_stop_record.stop_account = acct_id
    ws_stop_record.stop_check_number = ws_check_number
    ws_stop_record.stop_amount = ws_check_amount
    ws_stop_record.stop_payee = ws_payee_name
    ws_stop_record.stop_effective_date = ws_process_date
    ws_stop_record.stop_expiry_date = int(ws_process_date) + 180
    ws_stop_record.stop_status = 'A'
    write_stop_record()

def apply_stop_fee() -> None:
    """29300-apply_stop_fee."""
    logger.info("Executing apply_stop_fee")
    global ws_account_balance, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject
    ws_account_balance -= ws_stop_payment_fee
    update_account()
    ws_notif_type = 'stop_payment'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Stop payment placed on check #' + str(ws_check_number)
    send_notification()

def box_rental() -> None:
    """30100-box_rental."""
    logger.info("Executing box_rental")
    if ws_rental_request == 'Y':
        check_availability()
        if ws_box_available == 'Y':
            assign_box()
            create_rental_agreement()

def check_availability() -> None:
    """30110-check_availability."""
    logger.info("Executing check_availability")
    global ws_box_available, ws_assigned_box
    ws_box_available = 'N'
    ws_box_idx = 1
    while ws_box_idx <= ws_total_boxes:
        if box_status[ws_box_idx - 1] == 'A':
            if box_size[ws_box_idx - 1] == ws_requested_size:
                ws_box_available = 'Y'
                ws_assigned_box = ws_box_idx
                break
        ws_box_idx += 1

def assign_box() -> None:
    """30120-assign_box."""
    logger.info("Executing assign_box")
    box_status[ws_assigned_box - 1] = 'R'
    box_renter[ws_assigned_box - 1] = ws_customer_id
    box_rental_date[ws_assigned_box - 1] = ws_process_date

def create_rental_agreement() -> None:
    """30130-create_rental_agreement."""
    logger.info("Executing create_rental_agreement")
    global ws_rental_agreement
    ws_rental_agreement = WsRentalAgreement()
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_box_number = str(ws_assigned_box)
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_customer = ws_customer_id
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_start_date = ws_process_date
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_annual_fee = ws_box_size_fee[ws_requested_size]
    write_rental_record()

def perform_validation() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    logger.info("Executing perform_validation")
    validate_auth_code()
    if WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
        create_capture_record()

def validate_auth_code() -> None:
    """Validate authorization code."""
    logger.info("Executing validate_auth_code")
    global WS_AUTH_VALID, AUTH_SEARCH_KEY
    WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
    AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
    # Simulating READ auth_file
    auth_rec = WsAuthRec(auth_rec_status='P') # Assuming auth_rec and AuthFile exist
    if auth_rec:
        if auth_rec.auth_rec_status == 'P':
            WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'

def process_settlement() -> None:
    """Process settlement."""
    logger.info("Executing process_settlement")
    batch_transactions()
    calculate_fees()
    create_funding_record()
    send_settlement_file()

def batch_transactions() -> None:
    """Batch transactions."""
    logger.info("Executing batch_transactions")
    global WS_BATCH_TOTAL, WS_BATCH_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0")
    WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        # Simulating READ capture_file
        capture_record = CaptureRecord(capture_settled='N') # Assuming capture_record and CaptureFile exist
        if capture_record:
            if capture_record.capture_settled == 'N':
                WS_BATCH_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
                WS_BATCH_COUNT += 1
                capture_record.capture_settled = 'Y'
                # Simulating REWRITE capture_record FROM ws_capture_rec
                pass
        else:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def calculate_fees() -> None:
    """Calculate fees."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_fees")
    global WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE, WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE, WS_PROCESSOR_FEE, WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0175")
    WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0015")
    WS_PROCESSOR_FEE = Decimal(WS_BATCH_COUNT) * Decimal("0.10")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES = WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE + WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE + WS_PROCESSOR_FEE

def create_funding_record() -> None:
    """Create funding record."""
    logger.info("Executing create_funding_record")
    global WS_NET_FUNDING
    WS_NET_FUNDING = WS_BATCH_TOTAL - WS_TOTAL_FEES
    ws_funding_record = WsFundingRecord(funding_merchant=WS_MERCHANT_ID, funding_amount=WS_NET_FUNDING, funding_fees=WS_TOTAL_FEES, funding_date=int(WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 2) # Assuming WS_PROCESS_DATE can be converted to int
    # Simulating WRITE funding_record FROM ws_funding_record
    pass

def send_settlement_file() -> None:
    """Send settlement file."""
    logger.info("Executing send_settlement_file")
    #Simulating OPEN OUTPUT settlement_file
    write_settlement_header()
    write_settlement_detail()
    write_settlement_trailer()
    #Simulating CLOSE settlement_file
    pass

    pass

def write_settlement_detail() -> None:
    """Write settlement detail."""
    logger.info("Executing write_settlement_detail")
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #Simulating READ capture_file INTO ws_capture_rec
        capture_record = CaptureRecord(capture_settled = 'Y') # Assuming capture_record and CaptureFile exist
        if capture_record:
            if capture_record.capture_settled == 'Y':
                ws_settle_detail = WsSettleDetail(settle_record_type='D', settle_card=CAPTURE_CARD, settle_amount=CAPTURE_AMOUNT, settle_auth_code=CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE)
                #Simulating WRITE settlement_record FROM ws_settle_detail
                pass
        else:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def write_settlement_trailer() -> None:
    """Write settlement trailer."""
    logger.info("Executing write_settlement_trailer")
    ws_settle_trailer = WsSettleTrailer(settle_record_type='T', settle_total_count=WS_BATCH_COUNT, settle_total_amount=WS_BATCH_TOTAL)
    #Simulating WRITE settlement_record FROM ws_settle_trailer
    pass

def handle_chargeback() -> None:
    """Handle chargeback."""
    logger.info("Executing handle_chargeback")
    if WS_CHARGEBACK_REQUEST == 'Y':
        receive_chargeback()
        research_transaction()
        respond_to_chargeback()

def receive_chargeback() -> None:
    """Receive chargeback."""
    logger.info("Executing receive_chargeback")
    ws_chargeback_record = WsChargebackRecord(cb_card=WS_CB_CARD_NUMBER, cb_amount=WS_CB_AMOUNT, cb_reason=WS_CB_REASON_CODE, cb_case_id=WS_CB_CASE_NUMBER, cb_received_date=WS_PROCESS_DATE, cb_status='RECEIVED')
    #Simulating WRITE chargeback_record FROM ws_chargeback_record
    pass

def research_transaction() -> None:
    """Research transaction."""
    logger.info("Executing research_transaction")
    global WS_TRANS_FOUND, AUTH_SEARCH_KEY
    AUTH_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was WS_CB_AUTH_CODE
    #Simulating READ auth_file INTO ws_original_auth
    ws_original_auth = WsOriginalAuth() # Assuming ws_original_auth and AuthFile exist
    if ws_original_auth:
        WS_TRANS_FOUND = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_TRANS_FOUND = 'N'

def respond_to_chargeback() -> None:
    """Respond to chargeback."""
    logger.info("Executing respond_to_chargeback")
    if WS_TRANS_FOUND == 'Y':
        if WS_CB_REASON_CODE == '4837':
            no_card_present_response()
        elif WS_CB_REASON_CODE == '4853':
            merchandise_response()
        elif WS_CB_REASON_CODE == '4863':
            fraud_response()
        else:
            pass

def no_card_present_response() -> None:
    """Handle no card present chargeback."""
    logger.info("Executing no_card_present_response")
    pass

def merchandise_response() -> None:
    """Handle merchandise chargeback."""
    logger.info("Executing merchandise_response")
    pass

def fraud_response() -> None:
    """Handle fraud chargeback."""
    logger.info("Executing fraud_response")

@dataclass
class ChargebackData:
    """Chargeback data structure."""
    cb_action: str = ""
    cb_status: str = ""
    ws_avs_match: str = ""
    ws_cvv_match: str = ""
    ws_delivery_proof: str = ""
    ws_3ds_verified: str = ""
    ws_cb_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_merchant_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_cb_fee: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_fees_charged: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def no_card_present_response(chargeback_data: ChargebackData) -> None:
    """Handles the response when no card is present."""
    logger.info("Executing 31435-no_card_present_response")
    if chargeback_data.ws_avs_match == 'Y' and chargeback_data.ws_cvv_match == 'Y':
        chargeback_data.cb_action = 'REPRESENT'
        chargeback_data.cb_status = 'DISPUTE'
    else:
        accept_chargeback(chargeback_data)

def merchandise_response(chargeback_data: ChargebackData) -> None:
    """Handles the response for merchandise disputes."""
    logger.info("Executing 31436-merchandise_response")
    if chargeback_data.ws_delivery_proof == 'Y':
        chargeback_data.cb_action = 'REPRESENT'
        chargeback_data.cb_status = 'DISPUTE'
    else:
        accept_chargeback(chargeback_data)

def fraud_response(chargeback_data: ChargebackData) -> None:
    """Handles the response for fraud disputes."""
    logger.info("Executing 31437-fraud_response")
    if chargeback_data.ws_3ds_verified == 'Y':
        chargeback_data.cb_action = 'REPRESENT'
        chargeback_data.cb_status = 'DISPUTE'
    else:
        accept_chargeback(chargeback_data)

def general_response(chargeback_data: ChargebackData) -> None:
    """Handles the general response."""
    logger.info("Executing 31438-general_response")
    chargeback_data.cb_action = 'ACCEPT'
    accept_chargeback(chargeback_data)

def accept_chargeback(chargeback_data: ChargebackData) -> None:
    """Accepts the chargeback and updates balances."""
    logger.info("Executing 31439-accept_chargeback")
    chargeback_data.cb_status = 'ACCEPTED'
    chargeback_data.ws_merchant_balance -= chargeback_data.ws_cb_amount
    chargeback_data.ws_fees_charged += chargeback_data.ws_cb_fee

def date_utilities(date_ws: DateUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Performs date utility procedures."""
    logger.info("Executing 99000-date_utilities")
    get_current_date(date_ws)
    calculate_business_days(date_ws)
    check_holiday(date_ws, [])
    format_date(date_ws)

def get_current_date(date_ws: DateUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Gets the current date."""
    logger.info("Executing 99100-get_current_date")
    now = datetime.now()
    date_ws.ws_current_datetime = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    date_ws.ws_curr_year = str(now.year)
    date_ws.ws_curr_month = str(now.month)
    date_ws.ws_curr_day = str(now.day)
    date_ws.ws_work_year = date_ws.ws_curr_year
    date_ws.ws_work_month = date_ws.ws_curr_month
    date_ws.ws_work_day = date_ws.ws_curr_day

def calculate_business_days(date_ws: DateUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Calculates the number of business days between two dates."""
    logger.info("Executing 99200-calculate_business_days")
    date_ws.ws_business_days = 0
    calc_date = date_ws.ws_start_date
    while calc_date <= date_ws.ws_end_date:
        date_ws.ws_calc_date = calc_date
        check_if_business_day(date_ws, [])
        if date_ws.ws_is_business_day == 'Y':
            date_ws.ws_business_days += 1
        
        try:
            calc_date_dt = datetime.strptime(calc_date, '%Y%m%d').date()
            calc_date_dt = calc_date_dt.replace(day=calc_date_dt.day + 1)
            calc_date = calc_date_dt.strftime('%Y%m%d')
        except ValueError:
            calc_date_dt = datetime.strptime(calc_date, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
            calc_date_dt = calc_date_dt.replace(day=calc_date_dt.day + 1)
            calc_date = calc_date_dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
            
def check_if_business_day(date_ws: DateUtilitiesWorkStorage, holidays: list[str]) -> None:
    """Checks if a date is a business day."""
    logger.info("Executing 99210-check_if_business_day")
    date_ws.ws_is_business_day = 'Y'
    try:
        calc_date_dt = datetime.strptime(date_ws.ws_calc_date, '%Y%m%d').date()
    except ValueError:
        calc_date_dt = datetime.strptime(date_ws.ws_calc_date, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        
    date_ws.ws_day_of_week = calc_date_dt.weekday()
    
    if date_ws.ws_day_of_week == 5 or date_ws.ws_day_of_week == 6:
        date_ws.ws_is_business_day = 'N'
    
    check_holiday(date_ws, holidays)
    if date_ws.ws_is_holiday == 'Y':
        date_ws.ws_is_business_day = 'N'

def check_holiday(date_ws: DateUtilitiesWorkStorage, holidays: list[str]) -> None:
    """Checks if a date is a holiday."""
    logger.info("Executing 99300-check_holiday")
    date_ws.ws_is_holiday = 'N'
    
    try:
        calc_date_dt = datetime.strptime(date_ws.ws_calc_date, '%Y%m%d').strftime('%Y%m%d')
    except ValueError:
        calc_date_dt = datetime.strptime(date_ws.ws_calc_date, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

    for holiday_date in holidays:
        if holiday_date == calc_date_dt:
            date_ws.ws_is_holiday = 'Y'
            break

def format_date(date_ws: DateUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Formats the date based on the specified format."""
    logger.info("Executing 99400-format_date")
    if date_ws.ws_date_format == 'MMDDYYYY':
        date_ws.ws_formatted_date = f"{date_ws.ws_work_month}/{date_ws.ws_work_day}/{date_ws.ws_work_year}"
    elif date_ws.ws_date_format == 'DDMMYYYY':
        date_ws.ws_formatted_date = f"{date_ws.ws_work_day}/{date_ws.ws_work_month}/{date_ws.ws_work_year}"
    elif date_ws.ws_date_format == 'YYYYMMDD':
        date_ws.ws_formatted_date = f"{date_ws.ws_work_year}-{date_ws.ws_work_month}-{date_ws.ws_work_day}"

def string_utilities(string_ws: StringUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Performs string utility procedures."""
    logger.info("Executing 99500-string_utilities")
    left_trim(string_ws)
    right_trim(string_ws)
    pad_left(string_ws)
    pad_right(string_ws)

def left_trim(string_ws: StringUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Trims leading spaces from a string."""
    logger.info("Executing 99510-left_trim")
    string_ws.ws_lead_spaces = 0
    for char in string_ws.ws_input_string:
        if char == ' ':
            string_ws.ws_lead_spaces += 1
        else:
            break
    string_ws.ws_output_string = string_ws.ws_input_string[string_ws.ws_lead_spaces:]

def right_trim(string_ws: StringUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Trims trailing spaces from a string."""
    logger.info("Executing 99520-right_trim")
    string_ws.ws_string_len = len(string_ws.ws_input_string)
    string_ws.ws_trail_spaces = 0
    for char in reversed(string_ws.ws_input_string):
        if char == ' ':
            string_ws.ws_trail_spaces += 1
        else:
            break
    string_ws.ws_actual_len = string_ws.ws_string_len - string_ws.ws_trail_spaces
    string_ws.ws_output_string = string_ws.ws_input_string[:string_ws.ws_actual_len]

def pad_left(string_ws: StringUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Pads a string on the left with a specified character."""
    logger.info("Executing 99530-pad_left")
    string_ws.ws_pad_count = string_ws.ws_target_len - string_ws.ws_actual_len
    if string_ws.ws_pad_count > 0:
        string_ws.ws_output_string = string_ws.ws_pad_char * string_ws.ws_pad_count + string_ws.ws_input_string
    else:
        string_ws.ws_output_string = string_ws.ws_input_string

def pad_right(string_ws: StringUtilitiesWorkStorage) -> None:
    """Pads a string on the right with a specified character."""
    logger.info("Executing 99540-pad_right")
    string_ws.ws_pad_count = string_ws.ws_target_len - string_ws.ws_actual_len
    if string_ws.ws_pad_count > 0:
        string_ws.ws_output_string = string_ws.ws_input_string + string_ws.ws_pad_char * string_ws.ws_pad_count
    else:
        string_ws.ws_output_string = string_ws.ws_input_string

def process_data(ws_input_string: str, ws_output_string: str) -> str:
    """Process input data."""
    logger.info("Processing data")
    if ws_input_string:
        ws_output_string = ws_input_string
    return ws_output_string

def numeric_utilities() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    logger.info("Performing numeric utilities")
    round_amount()
    calculate_percentage()
    calculate_compound_interest()

def round_amount() -> None:
    """Round the amount."""
    logger.info("Rounding amount")
    global ws_rounded_amount, ws_input_amount
    ws_rounded_amount = ws_input_amount.quantize(Decimal("1"))

def calculate_percentage() -> None:
    """Calculate the percentage."""
    logger.info("Calculating percentage")
    global ws_percentage, ws_base_amount, ws_part_amount
    if ws_base_amount > Decimal("0"):
        ws_percentage = (ws_part_amount / ws_base_amount) * Decimal("100")
    else:
        ws_percentage = Decimal("0")

def calculate_compound_interest() -> None:
    """Calculate compound interest."""
    logger.info("Calculating compound interest")
    global ws_compound_result, ws_principal, ws_rate, ws_compounds_per_year, ws_years
    ws_compound_result = ws_principal * ((1 + ws_rate / ws_compounds_per_year) ** (ws_compounds_per_year * ws_years))

def file_utilities() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    logger.info("Performing file utilities")
    check_file_status()
    log_file_error()

def check_file_status() -> None:
    """Check file status."""
    logger.info("Checking file status")
    global ws_file_status, ws_file_result
    if ws_file_status == '00':
        ws_file_result = 'SUCCESS'
    elif ws_file_status == '10':
        ws_file_result = 'END OF FILE'
    elif ws_file_status == '21':
        ws_file_result = 'SEQUENCE ERROR'
    elif ws_file_status == '22':
        ws_file_result = 'DUPLICATE KEY'
    elif ws_file_status == '23':
        ws_file_result = 'RECORD NOT FOUND'
    elif ws_file_status == '24':
        ws_file_result = 'BOUNDARY VIOLATION'
    elif ws_file_status == '30':
        ws_file_result = 'PERMANENT ERROR'
    elif ws_file_status == '35':
        ws_file_result = 'FILE NOT FOUND'
    elif ws_file_status == '39':
        ws_file_result = 'ATTRIBUTE CONFLICT'
    elif ws_file_status == '41':
        ws_file_result = 'FILE ALREADY OPEN'
    elif ws_file_status == '42':
        ws_file_result = 'FILE NOT OPEN'
    elif ws_file_status == '43':
        ws_file_result = 'READ NOT DONE'
    elif ws_file_status == '44':
        ws_file_result = 'RECORD OVERFLOW'
    elif ws_file_status == '46':
        ws_file_result = 'READ ERROR'
    elif ws_file_status == '47':
        ws_file_result = 'INPUT FILE NOT OPEN'
    elif ws_file_status == '48':
        ws_file_result = 'OUTPUT FILE NOT OPEN'
    elif ws_file_status == '49':
        ws_file_result = 'I-O FILE NOT OPEN'
    else:
        ws_file_result = 'UNKNOWN ERROR'

    """Log file error."""
    logger.info("Logging file error")
    global ws_file_error_log, ws_file_name, ws_file_status, ws_file_result
    ws_file_error_log = FileErrorLog()

def error_handling() -> None:
    """Handle errors."""
    logger.info("Entering error_handling")
    format_error()
    display_error()
    write_error_log()

def format_error() -> None:
    """Format error message."""
    logger.info("Entering format_error")
    global ws_formatted_error, ws_error_code, ws_error_msg
    ws_formatted_error = f"ERROR: {ws_error_code} - {ws_error_msg}"

def display_error() -> None:
    """Display error message."""
    logger.info("Entering display_error")
    global ws_formatted_error
    print(ws_formatted_error)

def write_error_log() -> None:
    """Write error to log."""
    logger.info("Entering write_error_log")
    global ws_error_log_rec, ws_error_code, ws_error_msg, ws_program_name, ws_paragraph_name

    class WsErrorLogRec:
        """Error log record."""
        err_log_code: str = ""
        err_log_msg: str = ""
        err_log_timestamp: str = ""
        err_log_program: str = ""
        err_log_paragraph: str = ""

    ws_error_log_rec = WsErrorLogRec()

    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_code = ws_error_code
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_msg = ws_error_msg
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_timestamp = datetime.now().isoformat()
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_program = ws_program_name
    ws_error_log_rec.err_log_paragraph = ws_paragraph_name

    # Assume a file named "error_log.txt" exists and is open for writing
    with open("error_log.txt", "a") as f:
        f.write(f"{ws_error_log_rec.err_log_code},{ws_error_log_rec.err_log_msg},{ws_error_log_rec.err_log_timestamp},{ws_error_log_rec.err_log_program},{ws_error_log_rec.err_log_paragraph}")
")"

def project_investment_maturities(ws_eof_flag: str, ws_projection_date: str, ws_projected_inflows: Decimal, investment_file, ws_inv_rec) -> tuple[str, Decimal]:
    """Project investment maturities."""
    logger.info("Executing project_investment_maturities")
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        record = investment_file.read()
        if not record:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_inv_rec = record
            inv_maturity_date = ws_inv_rec.inv_maturity_date # Assuming ws_inv_rec is an object with attributes
            inv_par_value = ws_inv_rec.inv_par_value
            if inv_maturity_date <= ws_projection_date:
                ws_projected_inflows += inv_par_value
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    return ws_eof_flag, ws_projected_inflows

def manage_reserves(ws_reserve_deficiency: str) -> str:
    """Manage reserves."""
    logger.info("Executing manage_reserves")
    calculate_reserve_requirement()
    check_reserve_position()
    if ws_reserve_deficiency == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves()
    return ws_reserve_deficiency

def calculate_reserve_requirement(ws_total_deposits: Decimal, ws_reserve_ratio: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculate reserve requirement."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_reserve_requirement")
    ws_reserve_requirement = ws_total_deposits * ws_reserve_ratio
    return ws_reserve_requirement

def check_reserve_position(ws_fed_balance: Decimal, ws_reserve_requirement: Decimal) -> str:
    """Check reserve position."""
    logger.info("Executing check_reserve_position")
    ws_excess_reserves = ws_fed_balance - ws_reserve_requirement
    ws_reserve_deficiency: str
    if ws_excess_reserves < 0:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'N'
    return ws_reserve_deficiency

def cover_reserve_shortfall(ws_excess_reserves: Decimal) -> None:
    """Cover reserve shortfall."""
    logger.info("Executing cover_reserve_shortfall")
    ws_shortfall_amount = Decimal("0") - ws_excess_reserves
    borrow_fed_funds(ws_shortfall_amount=ws_shortfall_amount)

@dataclass
class WsFedFundsTransaction:
    """Fed funds transaction record."""
    ff_trans_type: str = ""
    ff_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ff_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ff_settle_date: str = ""
    ff_maturity_date: int = 0

def borrow_fed_funds(ws_process_date: str, ws_fed_funds_rate: Decimal, ws_shortfall_amount: Decimal, fed_funds_record) -> None:
    """Borrow fed funds."""
    logger.info("Executing borrow_fed_funds")
    ws_fed_funds_transaction = WsFedFundsTransaction()
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_trans_type = 'BORROW'
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_amount = ws_shortfall_amount
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_rate = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_settle_date = ws_process_date
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_maturity_date = int(ws_process_date) + 1
    fed_funds_record.write(ws_fed_funds_transaction)

def invest_excess_reserves(ws_excess_reserves: Decimal, ws_min_invest_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Invest excess reserves."""
    logger.info("Executing invest_excess_reserves")
    if ws_excess_reserves > ws_min_invest_amount:
        sell_fed_funds(ws_excess_reserves=ws_excess_reserves)

def sell_fed_funds(ws_process_date: str, ws_fed_funds_rate: Decimal, ws_excess_reserves: Decimal, fed_funds_record) -> None:
    """Sell fed funds."""
    logger.info("Executing sell_fed_funds")
    ws_fed_funds_transaction = WsFedFundsTransaction()
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_trans_type = 'SELL'
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_amount = ws_excess_reserves
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_rate = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_settle_date = ws_process_date
    ws_fed_funds_transaction.ff_maturity_date = int(ws_process_date) + 1
    fed_funds_record.write(ws_fed_funds_transaction)

def manage_investments() -> None:
    """Manage investments."""
    logger.info("Executing manage_investments")
    review_investment_portfolio()
    execute_investment_strategy()
    mark_to_market()

@dataclass
class WsInvRec:
    """Investment record."""
    inv_market_value: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    inv_yield: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    inv_duration: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    inv_cusip: str = ""
    inv_par_value: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    inv_book_value: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    inv_maturity_date: str = ""

def review_investment_portfolio(ws_eof_flag: str, investment_file, ws_inv_rec) -> None:
    """Review investment portfolio."""
    logger.info("Executing review_investment_portfolio")
    ws_investment_pool: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_avg_yield: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_avg_duration: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_total_yield: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_total_duration: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_inv_count: int = 0

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        record = investment_file.read()
        if not record:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_inv_rec = WsInvRec()
            ws_inv_rec.inv_market_value = Decimal("100") # PLACEHOLDER
            ws_inv_rec.inv_yield = Decimal("1") # PLACEHOLDER
            ws_inv_rec.inv_duration = Decimal("1") # PLACEHOLDER
            ws_investment_pool += ws_inv_rec.inv_market_value
            ws_total_yield += ws_inv_rec.inv_yield
            ws_total_duration += ws_inv_rec.inv_duration
            ws_inv_count += 1

    if ws_inv_count > 0:
        ws_avg_yield = ws_total_yield / ws_inv_count
        ws_avg_duration = ws_total_duration / ws_inv_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def execute_investment_strategy(ws_rate_outlook: str) -> None:
    """Execute investment strategy."""
    logger.info("Executing execute_investment_strategy")
    if ws_rate_outlook == 'RISING':
        shorten_duration()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'FALLING':
        extend_duration()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'STABLE':
        maintain_position()

def shorten_duration() -> None:
    """Shorten duration."""
    logger.info("Executing shorten_duration")
    print('STRATEGY: SHORTENING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def extend_duration() -> None:
    """Extend duration."""
    logger.info("Executing extend_duration")
    print('STRATEGY: EXTENDING PORTFOLIO DURATION')

def maintain_position() -> None:
    """Maintain position."""
    logger.info("Executing maintain_position")
    print('STRATEGY: MAINTAINING CURRENT POSITION')

def mark_to_market(ws_eof_flag: str, investment_file, ws_inv_rec) -> None:
    """Mark to market."""
    logger.info("Executing mark_to_market")
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        record = investment_file.read()
        if not record:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_inv_rec = WsInvRec()
            ws_inv_rec.inv_cusip = "test_cusip" # PLACEHOLDER
            ws_inv_rec.inv_par_value = Decimal("100") # PLACEHOLDER
            ws_inv_rec.inv_book_value = Decimal("90") # PLACEHOLDER
            get_market_price(inv_cusip=ws_inv_rec.inv_cusip)
            ws_market_price = Decimal("100") # PLACEHOLDER
            inv_market_value = ws_inv_rec.inv_par_value * ws_market_price / 100
            inv_unrealized_gl = inv_market_value - ws_inv_rec.inv_book_value
            # rewrite_investment_record(inv_market_value=inv_market_value, inv_unrealized_gl=inv_unrealized_gl)
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def get_market_price(inv_cusip: str) -> None:
    """Get market price."""
    logger.info("Executing get_market_price")
    ws_cusip_lookup = inv_cusip
    bondprice(ws_cusip_lookup=ws_cusip_lookup) # CALL 'BONDPRICE'

def bondprice(ws_cusip_lookup: str) -> Decimal:
    """Placeholder for bond price retrieval."""
    logger.info("Executing bondprice")
    return Decimal("100")

    """Review borrowing capacity."""
    logger.info("Executing review_borrowing_capacity")
    ws_borrowing_capacity: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_fhlb_capacity
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_repo_capacity
    ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_credit_line_avail
    return ws_borrowing_capacity

def optimize_funding_mix(ws_total_int_expense: Decimal, ws_total_deposits: Decimal, ws_wholesale_rate: Decimal) -> None:
    """Optimize funding mix."""
    logger.info("Executing optimize_funding_mix")
    if ws_total_deposits != Decimal("0"):
        ws_deposit_cost = ws_total_int_expense / ws_total_deposits * 100
    else:
        ws_deposit_cost = Decimal("0")
    if ws_deposit_cost > ws_wholesale_rate:
        print('CONSIDER WHOLESALE FUNDING')

WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
WS_PROCESS_DATE = ''
WS_CASH_POSITION = Decimal("0")

BORROWING_FILE = []
INVESTMENT_FILE = []
BORROWING_RECORD = []

def manage_maturities() -> None:
    """32530-manage_maturities."""
    logger.info("Executing manage_maturities")
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_BORROW_REC
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_BORROW_REC = BORROWING_FILE.pop(0)
            if WS_BORROW_REC.borrow_maturity <= Decimal(WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 7:
                rollover_decision()
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def rollover_decision() -> None:
    """32535-rollover_decision."""
    logger.info("Executing rollover_decision")
    global WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_BORROW_REC
    if WS_CASH_POSITION >= WS_BORROW_REC.borrow_amount:
        repay_borrowing()
    else:
        rollover_borrowing()

def repay_borrowing() -> None:
    """32536-repay_borrowing."""
    logger.info("Executing repay_borrowing")
    global WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_BORROW_REC
    WS_CASH_POSITION -= WS_BORROW_REC.borrow_amount
    WS_BORROW_REC.borrow_status = 'REPAID'
    #REWRITE borrowing_record FROM ws_borrow_rec
    BORROWING_RECORD.append(WS_BORROW_REC)

def rollover_borrowing() -> None:
    """32537-rollover_borrowing."""
    logger.info("Executing rollover_borrowing")
    global WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_CURRENT_RATE, WS_BORROW_REC
    WS_BORROW_REC.borrow_rollover_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_BORROW_REC.borrow_maturity = Decimal(int(WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30)
    WS_BORROW_REC.borrow_rate  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURRENT_RATE
    #REWRITE borrowing_record FROM ws_borrow_rec
    BORROWING_RECORD.append(WS_BORROW_REC)

    """33110-calculate_lcr."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_lcr")
    global WS_LCR_DENOMINATOR
    sum_hqla()
    calculate_net_outflows()
    if WS_LCR_DENOMINATOR > 0:
        calculate_lcr_ratio()

def sum_hqla() -> None:
    """33115-sum_hqla."""
    logger.info("Executing sum_hqla")
    global WS_LCR_NUMERATOR, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_INV_REC
    WS_LCR_NUMERATOR = Decimal("0")
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_INV_REC = INVESTMENT_FILE.pop(0)
            if WS_INV_REC.inv_hqla_level == '1':
                WS_LCR_NUMERATOR += WS_INV_REC.inv_market_value
            elif WS_INV_REC.inv_hqla_level == '2A':
                calculate_adjusted_value_2a()
            elif WS_INV_REC.inv_hqla_level == '2B':
                calculate_adjusted_value_2b()
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def calculate_adjusted_value_2a() -> None:
    """Calculate adjusted value for HQLA level 2A."""
    logger.info("Calculating adjusted value for HQLA level 2A")
    global WS_INV_REC, WS_LCR_NUMERATOR, WS_ADJUSTED_VALUE
    WS_ADJUSTED_VALUE = WS_INV_REC.inv_market_value * Decimal("0.85")
    WS_LCR_NUMERATOR += None  # TODO: was WS_ADJUSTED_VALUE

def calculate_adjusted_value_2b() -> None:
    """Calculate adjusted value for HQLA level 2B."""
    logger.info("Calculating adjusted value for HQLA level 2B")
    global WS_INV_REC, WS_LCR_NUMERATOR, WS_ADJUSTED_VALUE
    WS_ADJUSTED_VALUE = WS_INV_REC.inv_market_value * Decimal("0.50")
    WS_LCR_NUMERATOR += None  # TODO: was WS_ADJUSTED_VALUE

def calculate_net_outflows() -> None:
    """33116-calculate_net_outflows."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_net_outflows")
    global WS_TOTAL_OUTFLOWS, WS_TOTAL_INFLOWS, WS_RETAIL_OUTFLOW, WS_WHOLESALE_OUTFLOW, WS_LCR_DENOMINATOR, WS_STABLE_DEPOSITS, WS_LESS_STABLE_DEPOSITS, WS_OPERATIONAL_DEPOSITS, WS_NON_OPERATIONAL
    WS_TOTAL_OUTFLOWS = Decimal("0")
    WS_TOTAL_INFLOWS = Decimal("0")
    WS_RETAIL_OUTFLOW = WS_STABLE_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.03") + WS_LESS_STABLE_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.10")
    WS_WHOLESALE_OUTFLOW = WS_OPERATIONAL_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.25") + WS_NON_OPERATIONAL * Decimal("0.40")
    WS_TOTAL_OUTFLOWS += None  # TODO: was WS_RETAIL_OUTFLOW
    WS_TOTAL_OUTFLOWS += WS_WHOLESALE_OUTFLOW
    WS_LCR_DENOMINATOR = WS_TOTAL_OUTFLOWS - min(WS_TOTAL_INFLOWS, WS_TOTAL_OUTFLOWS * Decimal("0.75"))

def calculate_lcr_ratio() -> None:
    """Calculates the LCR ratio."""
    logger.info("Calculating the LCR ratio.")
    global WS_LCR_RATIO, WS_LCR_NUMERATOR, WS_LCR_DENOMINATOR
    WS_LCR_RATIO = (WS_LCR_NUMERATOR / WS_LCR_DENOMINATOR) * 100

    calculate_asf()
    calculate_rsf()
    if WS_NSFR_REQUIRED > 0:
        calculate_nsfr_ratio()

    WS_NSFR_AVAILABLE = Decimal("0")
    WS_NSFR_AVAILABLE += None  # TODO: was WS_TIER1_CAPITAL
    WS_NSFR_AVAILABLE += None  # TODO: was WS_TIER2_CAPITAL
    WS_STABLE_FUNDING = WS_RETAIL_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.95") + WS_WHOLESALE_DEPOSITS_1YR * Decimal("1.00") + WS_WHOLESALE_DEPOSITS_6M * Decimal("0.50")

@dataclass
class WsCfpDocument:

    cfp_total_sources: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    cfp_stress_needs: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def send_liquidity_alert(ws_alert_type: str) -> None:

def contingency_funding_plan() -> None:
    """Executes contingency funding plan."""
    logger.info("Executing contingency_funding_plan")
    assess_stress_scenario()
    identify_funding_sources()
    update_cfp_document()

def assess_stress_scenario() -> None:
    """Assesses the stress scenario."""
    logger.info("Executing assess_stress_scenario")
    ws_stress_level = "" # needs to be defined
    if ws_stress_level == 'LOW':
        ws_deposit_runoff = Decimal("0.05")
    elif ws_stress_level == 'MEDIUM':
        ws_deposit_runoff = Decimal("0.15")
    elif ws_stress_level == 'HIGH':
        ws_deposit_runoff = Decimal("0.30")
    elif ws_stress_level == 'SEVERE':
        ws_deposit_runoff = Decimal("0.50")
    else:
        ws_deposit_runoff = Decimal("0")

    ws_total_deposits = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_stressed_outflows = ws_total_deposits * ws_deposit_runoff

def identify_funding_sources() -> None:
    """Identifies potential funding sources."""
    logger.info("Executing identify_funding_sources")
    ws_available_funding = Decimal("0")
    ws_fhlb_capacity = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_repo_capacity = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_fed_discount_window = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_asset_sale_capacity = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined

    ws_available_funding += ws_fhlb_capacity
    ws_available_funding += ws_repo_capacity
    ws_available_funding += ws_fed_discount_window
    ws_available_funding += ws_asset_sale_capacity

    ws_stressed_outflows = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    global ws_cfp_status
    if ws_available_funding < ws_stressed_outflows:
        ws_cfp_status = 'INADEQUATE'
    else:
        ws_cfp_status = 'ADEQUATE'

def update_cfp_document() -> None:
    """Updates the CFP document."""
    logger.info("Executing update_cfp_document")
    ws_cfp_update_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    ws_available_funding = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_stressed_outflows = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined

    global cfp_overall_status
    global cfp_total_sources
    global cfp_stress_needs
    cfp_overall_status = ws_cfp_status
    cfp_total_sources = ws_available_funding
    cfp_stress_needs = ws_stressed_outflows
    #REWRITE cfp_record FROM ws_cfp_document. - skipping file IO

def calculate_tier1() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 1 capital."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_tier1")
    ws_tier1_capital = Decimal("0")
    ws_common_stock = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_retained_earnings = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_aoci = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_goodwill = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_intangibles = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_dta_deduction = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined

    ws_tier1_capital += ws_common_stock
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_retained_earnings
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_aoci
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_goodwill
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_intangibles
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_dta_deduction

def calculate_tier2() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 2 capital."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_tier2")
    ws_tier2_capital = Decimal("0")
    ws_sub_debt = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_alll_eligible = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined

    ws_tier2_capital += ws_sub_debt
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_alll_eligible

    global ws_total_capital
    ws_tier1_capital = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_total_capital = ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital

# SYNTAX: def c

ws_cet1_ratio = Decimal("0")
ws_capital_ratio = Decimal("0")
ws_leverage_ratio = Decimal("0")
ws_risk_weighted_assets = Decimal("0")

def calculate_ratios() -> None:
    """Calculates capital ratios."""
    logger.info("Executing calculate_ratios")
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_total_assets = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    global ws_cet1_ratio
    global ws_capital_ratio
    global ws_leverage_ratio
    ws_tier1_capital = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_total_capital = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined

    if ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0:
        ws_cet1_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
        ws_capital_ratio = (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_leverage_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100

def risk_weighted_assets() -> None:
    """Calculates risk-weighted assets."""
    logger.info("Executing risk_weighted_assets")
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = Decimal("0")
    credit_rwa()
    market_rwa()
    operational_rwa()

def credit_rwa() -> None:
    """Calculates credit risk-weighted assets."""
    logger.info("Executing credit_rwa")
    ws_cash_position = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_govt_securities = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_bank_deposits = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_residential_mortgages = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_commercial_loans = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    ws_consumer_loans = Decimal("0") # needs to be defined
    global ws_risk_weighted_assets

    ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * Decimal("0.20")
    ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * Decimal("0.50")
    ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * Decimal("1.00")
    ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * Decimal("1.00")

    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_cash_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_govt_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_bank_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_mortgage_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_commercial_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_consumer_rwa

logger = logging.getLogger('UNKNOWN')

@dataclass
class WsCapitalPlan:
    """Capital plan record."""
    plan_recommended_action: str = ""
    plan_gap_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def market_rwa(ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculate market RWA."""
    logger.info("Calculating market RWA")
    ws_market_rwa = ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_market_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

def operational_rwa(ws_gross_income: Decimal, ws_operational_factor: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculate operational RWA."""
    logger.info("Calculating operational RWA")
    ws_operational_rwa = ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * Decimal("12.5")
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_operational_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

def capital_planning(project_capital_needs, identify_capital_actions, update_capital_plan) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    logger.info("Performing capital planning")
    project_capital_needs()
    identify_capital_actions()
    update_capital_plan()

def project_capital_needs(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Project capital needs."""
    logger.info("Projecting capital needs")
    ws_projected_rwa = ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + ws_growth_rate)
    ws_required_capital = ws_projected_rwa * ws_target_ratio / 100
    ws_capital_gap = ws_required_capital - ws_total_capital
    return ws_projected_rwa, ws_required_capital, ws_capital_gap

def identify_capital_actions(ws_capital_gap: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal) -> str:
    """Identify capital actions."""
    logger.info("Identifying capital actions")
    ws_capital_action: str = ""
    if ws_capital_gap > 0:
        if ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:
            ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:
            ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'
    return ws_capital_action

def update_capital_plan(ws_capital_action: str, ws_capital_gap: Decimal, capital_plan_record: WsCapitalPlan) -> WsCapitalPlan:
    """Update capital plan."""
    logger.info("Updating capital plan")
    ws_plan_update_date: datetime = datetime.now()
    capital_plan_record.plan_recommended_action = ws_capital_action
    capital_plan_record.plan_gap_amount = ws_capital_gap
    return capital_plan_record

def stress_testing(run_baseline, run_adverse, run_severely_adverse, compile_results) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    logger.info("Performing stress testing")
    run_baseline()
    run_adverse()
    run_severely_adverse()
    compile_results()

def run_baseline(calculate_stress_impact) -> None:
    """Run baseline scenario."""
    logger.info("Running baseline scenario")
    ws_scenario_name: str = 'BASELINE'
    ws_rate_shock: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_gdp_change: Decimal = Decimal("2.50")
    ws_unemployment_rate: Decimal = Decimal("4.00")
    ws_housing_decline: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    calculate_stress_impact(ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline)

def run_adverse(calculate_stress_impact) -> None:
    """Run adverse scenario."""
    logger.info("Running adverse scenario")
    ws_scenario_name: str = 'ADVERSE'
    ws_rate_shock: Decimal = Decimal("2.00")
    ws_gdp_change: Decimal = Decimal("-1.50")
    ws_unemployment_rate: Decimal = Decimal("7.00")
    ws_housing_decline: Decimal = Decimal("-15.00")
    calculate_stress_impact(ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline)

def run_severely_adverse(calculate_stress_impact) -> None:
    """Run severely adverse scenario."""
    logger.info("Running severely adverse scenario")
    ws_scenario_name: str = 'severely_adverse'
    ws_rate_shock: Decimal = Decimal("3.00")
    ws_gdp_change: Decimal = Decimal("-6.00")
    ws_unemployment_rate: Decimal = Decimal("10.00")
    ws_housing_decline: Decimal = Decimal("-30.00")
    calculate_stress_impact(ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline)

def compile_results(ws_stress_pass_fail: str, remediation_actions) -> None:
    """Compile stress test results."""
    logger.info("Compiling stress test results")
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')
    if ws_stress_pass_fail == 'FAIL':
        remediation_actions()

def calculate_stress_impact(ws_scenario_name: str, ws_rate_shock: Decimal, ws_gdp_change: Decimal, ws_unemployment_rate: Decimal, ws_housing_decline: Decimal, ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal) -> str:
    """Calculate stress impact."""
    logger.info("Calculating stress impact")
    ws_credit_losses: Decimal = ws_loan_portfolio * ws_stress_lgd * ws_stress_pd
    ws_market_losses: Decimal = ws_trading_assets * ws_rate_shock / 100
    ws_stress_losses: Decimal = ws_credit_losses + ws_market_losses
    ws_stressed_capital: Decimal = ws_total_capital - ws_stress_losses
    ws_stressed_ratio: Decimal = (ws_stressed_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    ws_stress_pass_fail: str = ""
    if ws_stressed_ratio >= ws_min_capital_ratio:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'PASS'
    else:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'FAIL'
    return ws_stress_pass_fail

def remediation_actions(send_notification) -> None:
    """Take remediation actions."""
    logger.info("Taking remediation actions")
    ws_notif_type: str = 'stress_failure'
    ws_notif_channel: str = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject: str = 'URGENT: Stress test failure - action required'
    send_notification(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
